Helper: `fast_len(n)`: next power of two >=n.

`softmax_q(q)`: q = q - max; exp; sum; p = e/sum; np.clip.

`r_convolve_p(p, L)`: use real FFT. To improve speed, use `np.fft.rfft`. Return first 2N-1.
```
F = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
r = np.fft.irfft(F*F, L)[:2*N-1].real
r = np.maximum(r, 0.0)
return r
```

`corr_slice(p, w, L)`: w length 2N-1.
```
w_rev = w[::-1]
Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
Fw = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, L)
full = np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fw, L).real
return full[N-1:2*N-1][::-1]
```
Need ensure `full` length L; if L maybe smaller than 3N-1? Choose L>=3N-1. For r, L can be smaller; but use same L.

`softobjective_and_grad(q, beta, N)`:
- p=softmax(q)
- L=fast_len(3*N-1) maybe precomputed and passed.
- r=conv_p.
- m = r.max()
- shifted = beta*(r-m)
- For stability: if beta large, shifted <=0; max 0. exp, sum. If denom==0 (impossible) set denom=1.
- w = exp/denom
- soft = m + np.log(denom)/beta
- T = corr_slice(p,w,L)
- grad_p = 4*N*T  (since F=2N soft, ∂soft=2T)
- center grad_p? grad_q = p*(grad_p - grad_p @ p)
- objective = 2*N*soft
Return obj, grad_q, p, r, m, soft.

```
def _refine_p_subgrad(p, N, L, max_iter, timeout, start_time, best_score):
    p = p.copy()
    best = p.sum... objective exact via _p_exact_obj(p,N)
    for it:
       if time > timeout break
       r=_conv_p(p,L)
       m=r.max(); if not finite continue
       # choose active top K
       K = max(8, min(64, 2*N//16)) maybe
       idx = np.argpartition(r, -K)[-K:] if K < len(r) else np.arange(len(r))
       # maybe weight by softmax with beta_act = 2000 to smooth active:
       w = np.zeros(len(r)); w[idx]=1.0; w/=w.sum()
       T=_corr_T(p,w,L)
       grad=4*N*T
       eta = 0.5 / (np.max(grad)+1e-12)
       shift = np.dot(grad,p)
       factor = np.exp(-eta*(grad - shift))
       p_new = p*factor; p_new /= sum
       # random perturbation sometimes
       if it % 10 == 0:
           noise = np.random.normal(0, 0.01/(it/100+1), p.size)
           p_noisy = np.exp(np.log(p_new+1e-300)+0.1*noise); p_noisy/=sum
           obj_noisy = _p_exact_obj(p_noisy,N)
           if obj_noisy < best: p_new=p_noisy; best=obj_noisy
       obj = _p_exact_obj(p_new,N)
       if obj < best: p, best = p_new, obj
       else: # try smaller etas
           for _ in range(3): eta*=0.5; recompute
    return p,best
```

Start z:
```
def _make_z_start(q, kind):
   t=(np.arange(q)+0.5)/q
   if kind == 'linear': h=1+2*t
   elif kind == 'log': h=np.log(1+5*t)
   elif kind == 'pow_a': h=(t+0.05)**0.5
   elif kind == 'pow_b': h=(t+0.05)**1.5
   elif kind == 'exp': h=np.exp(2*t)
   elif kind == 'rect2': h=np.where(t<0.5,1,2)
   elif kind == 'step_line8': h=1+7*t
   elif 'sine': h=1+0.8*np.sin(2*np.pi*t+0.7)
   h = np.maximum(h, 1e-3)
   return np.log(h) - np.mean(np.log(h))
```

Let's think about L-BFGS objective gradient sign and softmax again.
`soft = m + log(den)/beta`.
`w = exp(beta*(r-m))/den`.
`∂ soft/∂ p_j = Σ w_k ∂r_k/∂p_j`.
`∂r_k/∂p_j = 2 p_{k-j}` for 0≤k-j<N.
`T_j = Σ_i p_i w_{i+j}` where i= k-j? k = i+j. So `∂soft = 2 T_j`.
`F=2N soft`. `grad_p=4N T`.
`grad_q = p*(grad_p - sum(grad_p*p))`.
This should be evaluated. Test uniform p length N, active all? If soft smooth, gradient maybe zero due uniform symmetry? Uniform p: r triangle; w weights not symmetric? r symmetric (for uniform yes). w symmetric. T_j = Σ_i p_i w_{j+i}. For uniform p=1/N, T_j=(1/N)Σ_{i=0}^{N-1} w_{j+i}. If w is not constant, T_j varies. Grad_p nonuniform; optimization moves away uniform. Good.
But note lags r not symmetric for nonuniform p? For convolution p*p, is r always symmetric? Wait earlier we said linear convolution of a with itself is not symmetric if a not palindromic. Let's test p=[0.2,0.8]: r=[0.04,0.32,0.64], not symmetric. Correct. So w non-symmetric. Our T formula correct? Derivative ∂r_k/∂p_j = 2p_{k-j} for k-j valid. For p=[p0,p1], r0=p0^2 derivative p0=2p0, p1=0; formula j=0,k=0:2p0; j=1,k=0:k-j=-1 invalid0. r1=2p0p1 derivative p0=2p1,p1=2p0. j=0,k=1:2p1; j=1,k=1:2p0. r2=p1^2 derivative p1=2p1; j=1,k=2:2p1. Good.
T_j formula with w: T0=w0p0+w1p1; ∂soft_p0=2T0? But sum w_k 2p_{k}: k=0 2p0 w0 + k=1 2p1 w1. yes. For p1 T1=w1p0+w2p1; ∂=2(w1p0+w2p1). yes.

Implement `_fast_len`:
```
def _fast_len(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    L=1
    while L < m: L <<= 1
    return L
```
For gradient L>=3N-1; for r only could be 2N-1. Use `L = _fast_len(3*N-1)` for both in vector; for exact objective use `_fast_len(2*N-1)` perhaps. Good.

`_conv_p(p,L)`:
```
N=len(p)
F=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
r=np.fft.irfft(F*F,L)[:2*N-1]
r=np.ascontiguousarray(r)
r[np.abs(r)<1e-15]=0? Not necessary.
np.maximum(r,0,out=r)
return r
```
`_corr_T(p,w,L)`:
```
N=len(p)
w_rev = w[::-1]
Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
Fw=np.fft.rfft(w_rev,L)
full=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fw,L)
return full[N-1:2*N-1][::-1]
```
Need real, clip tiny neg? T should nonneg; `np.maximum(T,0,out=T)`.

`_soft_obj_from_p(p,beta,L)`:

```
if den<=0 or not finite: w=1/len; soft=m
else: w=exps/den; soft=m+math.log(den)/beta
return 2*p.size*soft, r, w
```

```
    _set_opt_globals(N,beta)
    q0=np.asarray(q0,float).copy()

       res=minimize(_fun_global, q0, jac=_jac_global, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':int(maxiter), 'timeout':float(timeout), 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':20})
       xb=res.x if res.x is not None else q0
    except Exception:
       xb=q0
    return xb
```

```
elif len>N:

   idx=np.linspace(0,len,N+1).astype(int)
   p=np.array([arr[idx[i]:idx[i+1]].mean() if len>0 else0 ...]) # no closure? list comprehension okay not lambda.
```


`_refine_q_subgrad(q,N,beta,timeout, deadline, max_iter)`: No nested. Returns q,bestp,bestobj.
Need deadline passed. Use `_OPT_N`? We'll implement self-contained with L. Use same `_opt_eval`? It uses globals; could set `_set_opt_globals(N,beta)` and use `_opt_eval` to get grad. But `_opt_eval` returns soft objective; we want exact accept. Use it for gradient.
```
def _refine_q_subgrad(q,N,beta, max_iter, deadline):
  _set_opt_globals(N,beta)
  q=q.copy(); p=softmax(q); best=_p_exact_obj(p); base_q=q.copy()
  eta=0.5
  no_improve=0
  for it in range(max_iter):
    if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
    obj, grad_q = _opt_eval(q)
    if not finite: ...
    gmax=np.max(np.abs(grad_q))
    if gmax <= 1e-15: break
    # try steps
    accepted=False
    for attempt in range(5):
       q_new=q - eta*grad_q
       # periodically add noise
       if it % 20 == 10:
           q_new += 0.05*np.random.normal(size=N)
       q_new -= np.mean(q_new)
       p_new=_softmax(q_new)
       snew=p_new.sum(); p_new/=snew
       obj_new=_p_exact_obj(p_new)
       if obj_new < best:
          q=q_new; p=p_new; best=obj_new; accepted=True; no_improve=0; break
       eta *= 0.5
    if not accepted:
       no_improve +=1
       # reset occasionally to best base with noise
       if no_improve > 30:
          q = base_q + 0.1*np.random.normal(size=N); q-=mean
          # maybe update base if no improve? 
          no_improve=0
  return q,p,best
```
Need rng? Use global? We can use np.random.normal (global seed). Maybe set `np.random.seed(seed)`? No need. But deterministic? Could use `rng` created in propose and pass? Helper no closures; pass `rng_seed`? Simpler use `np.random.default_rng` inside? Creating many may be okay. But for reproducibility, pass `rng` to helper? Then helper uses `rng.normal`. OK.

Local block search top-level:
```
def _local_block_search(p,N,rng,deadline):
   p=p.copy(); best=_p_exact_obj(p); fail=0; L=_fast_len(2*N-1)
   for it in range(50000):
      if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
      sigma = 0.2 * (0.98**(fail//20)) + 0.01
      width = int(rng.integers(max(1,N//256), max(2,N//16)))
      start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1,N-width)))
      cand=p.copy()
      cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma))
      if rng.random()<0.3: cand = 0.5*(cand+cand[::-1])? That might destroy asymmetry; maybe skip mirror or with low prob.
      cand=np.maximum(cand,0); s=cand.sum(); if s<=0: cand=p; else cand/=s
      obj=_p_exact_obj(cand)
      if obj<best: p=cand; best=obj; fail=0; if it%100==0 print
      else: fail+=1
   return p,best
```
This is like previous but from good shape. Use deadline.

Need `_get_parent_p(N)` top-level:
```
def _parent_p(N):
   par = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
   if par is None: return None
   arr=np.asarray(par,float)
   arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
   if arr.size==0 or arr.sum<=0 or not finite: return None
   p=_downsample_sequence(arr,N)
   return p
```

Need top-level `_small_obj(x,n)` using global `_SMALL_N` to avoid closure.
```
_SMALL_N=0
def _set_small_n(n): global _SMALL_N; _SMALL_N=n
def _small_obj(x): n=_SMALL_N; a=np.clip(np.asarray(x,float),0,1e8); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-9: return 1e12; c=np.convolve(a,a); return 2.0*n*c.max()/(s*s)
```

Detailed helper code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_evolution

# globals
_OPT_N=0; _OPT_BETA=1.0; _OPT_L=0
_STEP_N=0; _STEP_Q=0; _STEP_BETA=1.0; _STEP_L=0
_SMALL_N=0
```

`_small_obj`:
```
def _small_obj(x):
    n=int(_SMALL_N)
    a=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1.0)
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return 1e100
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return 1e100
    val=2.0*float(n)*m/(s*s)
    return val if np.isfinite(val) else 1e100
```
Scaling in [0,1]; if optimum uses values >1? scale invariant, can clip to1. Good.

`_small_search`:
```
def _small_search(n, timeout, seed):
    if timeout <= 1: return None
    global _SMALL_N
    _SMALL_N=n
    bounds = [(0.0,1.0)]*n
    try:
       res=differential_evolution(_small_obj, bounds, seed=seed, maxiter=200, popsize=20, tol=1e-8, atol=1e-12, polish=False, timeout=float(timeout))
       if res.success or res.x is not None:
           x=np.asarray(res.x,float)
           obj=_small_obj(x)
           return x,obj
    except Exception:
       pass
    return None
```
Could timeout very small; DE may not respect? It should.

`_p_exact_obj_raw(a)`: for small list.
But for p normalized, use FFT.

`_p_to_list` scale max 1:
```
def _p_to_list(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    p=np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s <= 0: p=np.ones(p.size); s=1
    maxp=float(p.max())
    if maxp <= 0 or not math.isfinite(maxp): scale=1.0
    else: scale=1.0/maxp
    a=p*scale
    # Clip to [0,1000]
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in a]
```
If p length 0? not.

`_official_score(a)`:
```
def _official_score(a):
    try:
       val=float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a]))
       return val if math.isfinite(val) else np.inf
    except Exception:
       return np.inf
```

`_downsample_sequence` no lambda:
```
def _downsample_sequence(arr,N):
    arr=np.asarray(arr,float).ravel()
    arr=np.nan_to_num(arr,nan=0.0,posinf=0.0,neginf=0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    if arr.size==0: return np.full(N,1.0/N)
    if arr.size == N:
       p=arr.copy()
    elif arr.size > N:
       p=np.empty(N,float)
       edges=np.linspace(0,arr.size,N+1).astype(int)
       for i in range(N):
          seg=arr[edges[i]:edges[i+1]]
          p[i]=seg.mean() if seg.size else 0.0
    else:
       x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,arr.size)
       x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,N)
       p=np.interp(x_new,x_old,arr)
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.full(N,1.0/N)
    p/=s
    return p
```

`_parent_p(N)`: use globals.

Step starts:
```
def _make_h_start(q, kind):
    t=(np.arange(q)+0.5)/float(q)
    if kind == 'linear2': h=1.0+2.0*t
    elif kind == 'linear6': h=1.0+6.0*t
    elif kind == 'pow05': h=(t+0.05)**0.5
    elif kind == 'pow15': h=(t+0.05)**1.5
    elif kind == 'exp2': h=np.exp(2.0*t)
    elif kind == 'recip': h=1.0/(t+0.2)
    elif kind == 'sine1': h=1.0+0.6*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*t+0.8)
    elif kind == 'sine2': h=1.0+0.4*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*t+0.5)+0.25*np.sin(4.0*np.pi*t+0.1)
    else: h=1.0+2.0*t
    return np.maximum(h,1e-3)
```

`_init_step_p(N,q,kind)`:
```
h=_make_h_start(q,kind)
rep=max(1,N//q)
p=np.repeat(h,rep)
if p.size < N: p=np.concatenate((p, np.repeat(h[-1], N-p.size)))
elif p.size > N: p=p[:N]
p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s<=0: p=np.ones(N)/N; else p/=s
return p
```

`_init_power_p(N, alpha, decreasing=False)`:
```
x=np.linspace(0,1,N,endpoint=False)+0.5/N
if decreasing: x=1-x
p=(x+1e-3)**alpha
p=np.maximum(p,1e-6); p/=p.sum(); return p
```

`_soft_obj_from_p` returns objective maybe and r,w. For step only obj.
```
def _soft_obj_from_p(p,beta,L):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    N=p.size
    r=_conv_p(p,L)
    m=float(r.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m <=0:
       return 2.0*N*float(1.0/(2*N-1)), r, np.ones(2*N-1)/(2*N-1)
    shifted=beta*(r-m)
    shifted=np.clip(shifted, -745.0, 0.0)
    exps=np.exp(shifted)
    den=float(exps.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(den) or den <=0:
       w=np.full(r.size, 1.0/r.size)
       soft=m
    else:
       w=exps/den
       soft=m + math.log(den)/beta
    return 2.0*N*soft, r, w
```

```
    if not finite: gq=zeros
    obj=2N soft

```

```
def _run_step_min(z0,N,q,beta,maxiter,timeout):
   _set_step_globals(N,q,beta)
   z0=np.asarray(z0,float).copy()
   try:
      res=minimize(_step_fun, z0, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':int(maxiter), 'timeout':float(timeout), 'ftol':1e-10, 'gtol':1e-8, 'eps':1e-4, 'maxls':20})
      return res.x
   except Exception:
      return z0
```

`_refine_q_subgrad(q,N,beta,max_iter,deadline,rng)`:
Use global opt eval. But `_opt_eval` uses current globals; set. We need exact accept. L for exact. Use `_p_exact_obj` with own L fast 2N.
```
def _refine_q_subgrad(q,N,beta,max_iter,deadline,rng):
   _set_opt_globals(N,beta)
   q=np.asarray(q,float).copy(); p=_softmax(q); best=_p_exact_obj(p)
   base_q=q.copy(); eta=0.5; fail=0
   L_exact=_fast_len(2*N-1) # _p_exact_obj computes itself
   for it in range(max_iter):
      if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
      try: obj,gq=_opt_eval(q)
      except: break
      gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(gq)))
      if not math.isfinite(gmax) or gmax < 1e-14: break
      accepted=False
      for _ in range(6):
         qn=q - eta*gq
         if fail > 20 and rng.random()<0.2:
             qn = base_q + (0.1 + 0.05*rng.random())*rng.normal(size=N)
         qn -= float(np.mean(qn))
         pn=_softmax(qn)
         s=pn.sum(); if s>0: pn/=s
         on=_p_exact_obj(pn)
         if on < best:
             q=qn; p=pn; best=on; eta=min(1.0, eta*1.25); fail=0; accepted=True; break
         eta *= 0.5
      if not accepted:
         fail +=1
         if fail == 25:
             base_q = q.copy(); fail=0
   return q,p,best
```
This can be heavy if exact each attempt. max_iter 200, attempts up to6 =>1200 exact FFTs, N1024 okay. timeout controls.

`_local_block_search(p,N,rng,deadline)`:
```
def _local_block_search(p,N,rng,deadline):
   p=p.copy(); best=_p_exact_obj(p); fail=0
   max_iter=50000
   for it in range(max_iter):
      if time.monotonic()>=deadline: break
      sigma=0.25*math.exp(-it/3000.0)+0.02
      lo=max(1,N//512); hi=max(lo+1,N//16)
      width=int(rng.integers(lo, hi, endpoint=True))
      width=min(max(1,width),N)
      start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,N-width+1)))
      cand=p.copy()
      cand[start:start+width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
      if rng.random()<0.2:
          # occasional global shape tweak: multiply by smooth sinus
          x=np.linspace(0,1,N)
          cand *= 1.0 + 0.1*math.sin(2*np.pi*x*rng.integers(1,5)+rng.random()*6)
      cand=np.maximum(cand,0); s=cand.sum()
      if s>0 and math.isfinite(s): cand/=s
      obj=_p_exact_obj(cand)
      if obj < best:
         p=cand; best=obj; fail=0
      else: fail+=1
   return p,best
```
But N local with no helper to create x each iter expensive. Skip sine tweak or do every 100 with precomputed? We can keep simple.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start + max(0.0, float(budget_s)-15.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_p=None; best_obj=np.inf; best_raw=None; best_raw_score=np.inf
    # small n search

          score=_official_score([float(v) for v in raw])
          if score < best_raw_score: ...
    # parent start
    parentp=_parent_p(1024)
    if parentp is not None:
       obj=_p_exact_obj(parentp)

    # if best_p None: uniform 128
    if best_p is None: best_p=np.ones(128, float)/128; best_obj=_p_exact_obj(best_p)
    print initial
```

`_fast_len` returns int.

`_softmax`: if q empty? return empty. But N>0.
```
def _softmax(q):
   q=np.asarray(q,float)
   if q.size==0: return q
   m=np.max(q)
   if not np.isfinite(m):
      q=np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
      m=np.max(q)
   qc=q-m
   qc=np.clip(qc, -700.0, 0.0)
   e=np.exp(qc)
   s=e.sum()
   if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.full(q.size, 1.0/q.size)
   return e/s
```

`_conv_p`:
```
def _conv_p(p,L):
   N=p.size
   F=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   o=np.fft.irfft(F*F,L)
   r=o[:2*N-1]
   r=np.maximum(r, 0.0)
   return r
```
Tiny imag? irfft real.

`_corr_T`:
```
def _corr_T(p,w,L):
   N=p.size
   w_rev=w[::-1]
   Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   Fw=np.fft.rfft(w_rev,L)
   full=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fw,L)
   t=full[N-1:2*N-1][::-1]
   t=np.maximum(t,0.0)
   return t
```

`_soft_obj_from_p`: compute.

`_set_opt_globals`, `_opt_eval`.

In `_opt_eval`, if `_OPT_N <=0` return inf zeros? Not called.
```
def _opt_eval(q):
    N=_OPT_N
    if N <=0: return np.inf, np.zeros_like(np.asarray(q))
    beta=_OPT_BETA; L=_OPT_L
    p=_softmax(q)
    r=_conv_p(p,L)
    m=float(np.max(r)) if r.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m <=0.0:
       obj=0.0
       g=np.zeros(N)
       return obj,g
    shifted=beta*(r-m)
    shifted=np.clip(shifted,-745.0,0.0)
    exps=np.exp(shifted)
    den=float(np.sum(exps))
    if not math.isfinite(den) or den <=0:
       w=np.full(r.size, 1.0/r.size)
       soft=m
    else:
       w=exps/den
       soft=m + math.log(den)/beta
    T=_corr_T(p,w,L)
    grad_p=4.0*float(N)*T
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_p)):
       grad_p=np.where(np.isfinite(grad_p), grad_p, 0.0)
    shift=float(np.dot(grad_p,p))
    gq=p*(grad_p-shift)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gq)):
       gq=np.where(np.isfinite(gq), gq, 0.0)
    obj=2.0*float(N)*soft
    if not math.isfinite(obj): obj=np.inf
    return float(obj), gq
```
If obj inf, gradient maybe. L-BFGS may fail; catch.

```
def _fun_global(q):
    obj, g = _opt_eval(q)
    return obj
```

`_p_exact_obj`: For p normalized. If p.size ==0 return inf. If direct for N<=1000 maybe; direct for 1000 -> 4M, okay. Use threshold 700 to be safe for many calls. FFT for >700.
```
def _p_exact_obj(p):
    N=p.size
    if N==0: return inf
    if N <= 700:
       c=np.convolve(p,p)
    else:
       L=_fast_len(2*N-1); c=_conv_p(p,L)
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(m): return inf
    val=2.0*float(N)*m
    return val if math.isfinite(val) else inf
```
Since p sum1. If p not sum1 due numerical, objective should use sum? In internal p normalized. For final candidate p normalized. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_evolution
except Exception:
    minimize = None
    differential_evolution = None

# Global state for callable objective wrappers used by SciPy.
_OPT_N = 0
_OPT_BETA = 1.0
_OPT_L = 0

_STEP_N = 0
_STEP_Q = 0
_STEP_BETA = 1.0
_STEP_L = 0

_SMALL_N = 0


def _fast_len(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    n = 1
    while n < m:
        n <<= 1
    return n


def _softmax(q):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=float)
    if q.size == 0:
        return q
    q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    m = float(np.max(q))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return np.full(q.size, 1.0 / float(q.size))
    z = q - m
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(q.size, 1.0 / float(q.size))
    return e / s


def _conv_p(p, L):
    N = int(p.size)
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    out = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)
    r = out[:2 * N - 1]
    r = np.ascontiguousarray(r, dtype=float)
    np.maximum(r, 0.0, out=r)
    return r


def _corr_T(p, w, L):
    # Returns T_j = sum_i p_i w_{j+i}, j=0..N-1.
    N = int(p.size)
    w_rev = w[::-1]
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, L)
    full = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fw, L)
    t = full[N - 1:2 * N - 1][::-1]
    t = np.ascontiguousarray(t, dtype=float)
    np.maximum(t, 0.0, out=t)
    return t


def _soft_obj_from_p(p, beta, L):
    N = int(p.size)
    r = _conv_p(p, L)
    m = float(np.max(r)) if r.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        m = 1.0 / max(1, 2 * N - 1)
        w = np.full(max(1, 2 * N - 1), 1.0 / max(1, 2 * N - 1))
        soft = m
    else:
        shifted = beta * (r - m)
        shifted = np.clip(shifted, -745.0, 0.0)
        exps = np.exp(shifted)
        den = float(np.sum(exps))
        if not math.isfinite(den) or den <= 0.0:
            rlen = int(r.size)
            w = np.full(rlen, 1.0 / max(1, rlen))
            soft = m
        else:
            w = exps / den
            soft = m + math.log(den) / beta
    obj = 2.0 * float(N) * soft
    if not math.isfinite(obj):
        obj = np.inf
    return float(obj), r, w


def _set_opt_globals(N, beta):
    global _OPT_N, _OPT_BETA, _OPT_L
    _OPT_N = int(N)
    _OPT_BETA = float(beta)
    _OPT_L = _fast_len(3 * N - 1)


def _opt_eval(q):
    N = _OPT_N
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=float)
    if N <= 0:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(q.size, dtype=float)
    beta = _OPT_BETA
    L = _OPT_L

    p = _softmax(q)
    r = _conv_p(p, L)
    m = float(np.max(r)) if r.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(N, dtype=float)

    shifted = beta * (r - m)
    shifted = np.clip(shifted, -745.0, 0.0)
    exps = np.exp(shifted)
    den = float(np.sum(exps))
    if not math.isfinite(den) or den <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(r.size, 1.0 / max(1, r.size))
        soft = m
    else:
        w = exps / den
        soft = m + math.log(den) / beta

    T = _corr_T(p, w, L)
    # F = 2N * softmax(r), d(softmax)/dp = 2*T, so dF/dp = 4N*T.
    grad_p = 4.0 * float(N) * T
    grad_p = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_p), grad_p, 0.0)
    shift = float(np.dot(grad_p, p))
    gq = p * (grad_p - shift)
    gq = np.where(np.isfinite(gq), gq, 0.0)

    obj = 2.0 * float(N) * soft
    if not math.isfinite(obj):
        obj = np.inf
    return float(obj), gq.astype(float, copy=False)


def _fun_global(q):
    obj, _g = _opt_eval(q)
    return obj


def _jac_global(q):
    _obj, g = _opt_eval(q)
    return g


def _run_vector_lbfgs(q0, N, beta, maxiter, timeout):
    if minimize is None:
        return np.asarray(q0, dtype=float).copy()
    _set_opt_globals(N, int(beta))
    x0 = np.asarray(q0, dtype=float).copy()
    x0 = np.nan_to_num(x0, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    x0 -= float(np.mean(x0))
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _fun_global,
            x0,
            jac=_jac_global,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "timeout": float(max(1e-6, timeout)),
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
                "maxls": 20,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        xb = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float) if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None else x0
    except Exception:
        xb = x0
    xb = np.nan_to_num(xb, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    xb -= float(np.mean(xb))
    return xb


def _set_step_globals(N, q, beta):
    global _STEP_N, _STEP_Q, _STEP_BETA, _STEP_L
    _STEP_N = int(N)
    _STEP_Q = int(q)
    _STEP_BETA = float(beta)
    _STEP_L = _fast_len(2 * N - 1)


def _step_p(z):
    N = _STEP_N
    Q = _STEP_Q
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    zc = z - float(np.max(z))
    h = np.exp(np.clip(zc, -700.0, 0.0))
    h = np.maximum(h, 1e-300)
    rep = max(1, N // Q)
    p = np.repeat(h, rep)
    if p.size < N:
        p = np.concatenate((p, np.repeat(h[-1], N - p.size)))
    elif p.size > N:
        p = p[:N]
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N))
    return p / s


def _step_fun(z):
    p = _step_p(z)
    obj, _r, _w = _soft_obj_from_p(p, _STEP_BETA, _STEP_L)
    return obj


def _run_step_min(z0, N, q, beta, maxiter, timeout):
    if minimize is None:
        return np.asarray(z0, dtype=float).copy()
    _set_step_globals(N, q, beta)
    x0 = np.asarray(z0, dtype=float).copy()
    x0 = np.nan_to_num(x0, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    x0 -= float(np.mean(x0))
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _step_fun,
            x0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "timeout": float(max(1e-6, timeout)),
                "ftol": 1e-10,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
                "eps": 1e-4,
                "maxls": 20,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        xb = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float) if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None else x0
    except Exception:
        xb = x0
    xb = np.nan_to_num(xb, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    xb -= float(np.mean(xb))
    return xb


def _set_small_n(n):
    global _SMALL_N
    _SMALL_N = int(n)


def _small_obj(x):
    n = int(_SMALL_N)
    if n <= 0:
        return 1e100
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 1e-12 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return 1e100
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return 1e100
    val = 2.0 * float(n) * m / (s * s)
    return val if math.isfinite(val) else 1e100


def _small_search(n, timeout, seed):
    if differential_evolution is None or n <= 0 or timeout <= 0.0:
        return None
    _set_small_n(n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * int(n)
    try:
        res = differential_evolution(
            _small_obj,
            bounds,
            seed=int(seed) % (2**31 - 1),
            maxiter=220,
            popsize=18,
            tol=1e-8,
            atol=1e-12,
            polish=False,
            timeout=float(timeout),
            disp=False,
        )
        if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
            x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
            obj = float(_small_obj(x))
            return x, obj
    except Exception:
        return None
    return None


def _p_exact_obj(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    N = int(p.size)
    if N <= 0:
        return np.inf
    if N <= 700:
        c = np.convolve(p, p)
    else:
        c = _conv_p(p, _fast_len(2 * N - 1))
    m = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    val = 2.0 * float(N) * m
    return val if math.isfinite(val) else np.inf


def _p_to_list(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        p = np.ones(max(1, p.size), dtype=float)
        s = float(p.size)
    p = p / s
    mx = float(np.max(p))
    if mx <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(mx):
        scale = 1.0
    else:
        scale = 1.0 / mx
    a = p * scale
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def _official_score(a):
    try:
        val = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a]))
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    return val if math.isfinite(val) else np.inf


def _upsample_p(p, M):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    M = int(M)
    if M <= 0:
        return p.copy()
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(M, 1.0 / float(M))
    p = p / s
    if p.size == M:
        return p.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, M)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    t = float(np.sum(out))
    if t <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(t):
        out = np.full(M, 1.0 / float(M))
    else:
        out = out / t
    return out


def _downsample_sequence(arr, N):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=float).ravel()
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    N = int(N)
    if N <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N))
    if arr.size == N:
        p = arr.copy()
    elif arr.size > N:
        p = np.empty(N, dtype=float)
        edges = np.linspace(0, arr.size, N + 1).astype(int)
        for i in range(N):
            seg = arr[edges[i]:edges[i + 1]]
            p[i] = float(np.mean(seg)) if seg.size else 0.0
    else:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N)
        p = np.interp(x_new, x_old, arr)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N))
    return p / s


def _parent_p(N):
    par = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if par is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(par, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        return None
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    if float(np.sum(arr)) <= 0.0:
        return None
    return _downsample_sequence(arr, int(N))


def _make_h_start(q, kind):
    q = int(q)
    t = (np.arange(q) + 0.5) / float(max(1, q))
    if kind == "linear2":
        h = 1.0 + 2.0 * t
    elif kind == "linear6":
        h = 1.0 + 6.0 * t
    elif kind == "pow05":
        h = (t + 0.05) ** 0.5
    elif kind == "pow15":
        h = (t + 0.05) ** 1.5
    elif kind == "pow25":
        h = (t + 0.05) ** 2.5
    elif kind == "exp2":
        h = np.exp(2.0 * t)
    elif kind == "recip":
        h = 1.0 / (t + 0.2)
    elif kind == "sine1":
        h = 1.0 + 0.6 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t + 0.8)
    elif kind == "sine2":
        h = 1.0 + 0.4 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t + 0.5) + 0.25 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * t + 0.1)
    else:
        h = 1.0 + 2.0 * t
    return np.maximum(h, 1e-3)


def _init_step_p(N, q, kind):
    N = int(N)
    q = int(q)
    h = _make_h_start(q, kind)
    rep = max(1, N // q)
    p = np.repeat(h, rep)
    if p.size < N:
        p = np.concatenate((p, np.repeat(h[-1], N - p.size)))
    elif p.size > N:
        p = p[:N]
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N))
    return p / s


def _init_power_p(N, alpha, decreasing=False):
    N = int(N)
    if N <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N, endpoint=False) + 0.5 / float(N)
    if decreasing:
        x = 1.0 - x
    p = (x + 1e-3) ** float(alpha)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-9)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N))
    return p / s


def _init_exp_p(N, alpha, decreasing=False):
    N = int(N)
    if N <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N, endpoint=False) + 0.5 / float(N)
    if decreasing:
        x = 1.0 - x
    p = np.exp(float(alpha) * x)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-12)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N))
    return p / s


def _init_sine_p(N, harm, amp, phase):
    N = int(N)
    if N <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N, endpoint=False)
    p = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.sin(float(harm) * 2.0 * np.pi * x + float(phase))
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-3)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N))
    return p / s


def _refine_q_subgrad(q, N, beta, max_iter, deadline, rng):
    N = int(N)
    if N <= 0:
        return q, np.asarray(q, dtype=float), np.inf
    _set_opt_globals(N, beta)
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=float).copy()
    q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    q -= float(np.mean(q))
    p = _softmax(q)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    p = p / s
    best = float(_p_exact_obj(p))
    base_q = q.copy()
    eta = 0.25
    fail = 0
    max_iter = int(max_iter)

    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        try:
            _soft_obj, gq = _opt_eval(q)
        except Exception:
            break
        gq = np.asarray(gq, dtype=float)
        gq = np.where(np.isfinite(gq), gq, 0.0)
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(gq))) if gq.size else 0.0
        if not math.isfinite(gmax) or gmax < 1e-14:
            break

        accepted = False
        for _attempt in range(7):
            qn = q - eta * gq
            if fail > 30 and rng.random() < 0.25:
                noise_scale = 0.08 + 0.12 * float(rng.random())
                qn = base_q + noise_scale * rng.normal(size=N)
            qn = np.nan_to_num(qn, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            qn -= float(np.mean(qn))
            pn = _softmax(qn)
            sp = float(np.sum(pn))
            if sp > 0.0 and math.isfinite(sp):
                pn = pn / sp
                on = float(_p_exact_obj(pn))
                if math.isfinite(on) and on < best:
                    q = qn
                    p = pn
                    best = on
                    eta = min(0.8, eta * 1.3)
                    fail = 0
                    accepted = True
                    break
            eta *= 0.5

        if not accepted:
            fail += 1
            if fail == 40:
                base_q = q.copy()
                fail = 0

    p = _softmax(q)
    sp = float(np.sum(p))
    if sp > 0.0 and math.isfinite(sp):
        p = p / sp
    else:
        p = np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N))
    return q, p, float(_p_exact_obj(p))


def _local_block_search(p, N, rng, deadline):
    N = int(N)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).copy()
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        p = np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N))
    else:
        p = p / s
    best = float(_p_exact_obj(p))
    fail = 0

    for it in range(100000):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        sigma = 0.25 * math.exp(-float(it) / 4000.0) + 0.015
        lo = max(1, N // 512)
        hi = max(lo + 1, N // 16)
        if hi <= lo:
            width = lo
        else:
            width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi, endpoint=True))
        width = int(min(max(1, width), N))
        stop = max(1, N - width + 1)
        start = int(rng.integers(0, stop))

        cand = p.copy()
        multiplier = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
        cand[start:start + width] *= multiplier

        if rng.random() < 0.18 and it % 7 == 0:
            # Smooth global modulation, still nonnegative.
            x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, N)
            mode = int(rng.integers(1, 6))
            ph = float(rng.random() * 6.283185307179586)
            scale = 1.0 + 0.08 * float(rng.random()) * np.sin(mode * 2.0 * np.pi * x + ph)
            cand *= scale

        cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
        cs = float(np.sum(cand))
        if cs > 0.0 and math.isfinite(cs):
            cand = cand / cs
            obj = float(_p_exact_obj(cand))
            if math.isfinite(obj) and obj < best:
                p = cand
                best = obj
                fail = 0
            else:
                fail += 1
        else:
            fail += 1

    return p, best


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    hard_deadline = start + max(0.0, budget - 6.0)
    work_deadline = start + max(0.0, budget - 35.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**31 - 1))

    best_p = None
    best_obj = np.inf
    small_ps = []

    def _consider_p(p):
        nonlocal best_p, best_obj
        if p is None:
            return
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
        p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(p))
        if p.size == 0 or s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
            return
        p = p / s
        obj = float(_p_exact_obj(p))
        if math.isfinite(obj) and obj < best_obj:
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_obj = obj

    # Small exact/low-dimensional global searches.  These can reveal short
    # sequences with unusually good discrete behavior.
    small_ns = (4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10)
    for n in small_ns:
        if time.monotonic() >= work_deadline - 60.0:
            break
        rem = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
        timeout = min(8.0, max(1.0, rem - 30.0))
        res = _small_search(n, timeout, seed + 17 + 3 * n)
        if res is not None:
            x, obj = res
            if math.isfinite(obj):
                x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
                s = float(np.sum(x))
                if s > 0.0:
                    p = (x / s)
                    p = p / float(np.sum(p))
                    _consider_p(p)
                    small_ps.append(p.copy())

    # Parent sequence, if available.
    par = _parent_p(1024)
    if par is not None:
        _consider_p(par)

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = np.ones(256, dtype=float) / 256.0
        best_obj = float(_p_exact_obj(best_p))

    print(f"[t={time.monotonic()-start:7.2f}] initial best objective = {best_obj:.9f}, len={best_p.size if best_p is not None else 0}", flush=True)

    # Piecewise-constant low-dimensional optimization.  This is powerful for
    # step-shaped profiles and supplies strong starts for full-vector search.
    if hard_deadline - time.monotonic() > 0.0 and minimize is not None:
        base_kinds = [
            "linear2", "linear6", "pow05", "pow15", "exp2",
            "recip", "sine1", "sine2"
        ]
        step_schedule = [
            (8, 512, 4, 1),
            (16, 1024, 6, 2),
            (32, 2048, 5, 2),
        ]
        for q, Nq, nkinds, npass in step_schedule:
            if time.monotonic() >= work_deadline - 45.0:
                break
            for kind in base_kinds[:nkinds]:
                if time.monotonic() >= work_deadline - 25.0:
                    break
                h0 = _make_h_start(q, kind)
                h0 = np.maximum(h0, 1e-3)
                z0 = np.log(h0)
                z0 -= float(np.mean(z0))
                z = z0.copy()
                for pass_idx in range(npass):
                    if time.monotonic() >= work_deadline - 15.0:
                        break
                    beta = 300.0 if pass_idx == 0 else 1000.0
                    rem = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
                    timeout = min(12.0, max(1.0, rem - 10.0))
                    z = _run_step_min(z, Nq, q, beta, 70, timeout)
                    p = _step_p(z)
                    _consider_p(p)

        print(f"[t={time.monotonic()-start:7.2f}] after step phases objective = {best_obj:.9f}, len={best_p.size if best_p is not None else 0}", flush=True)

    # Full vector optimization from several structured starts.
    if minimize is not None and hard_deadline - time.monotonic() > 0.0:
        vector_schedule = [
            # N, number of starts, beta low, beta high, timeout per pass
            (128, 4, 300.0, 900.0, 12.0),
            (256, 3, 400.0, 1100.0, 20.0),
            (512, 2, 600.0, 1500.0, 35.0),
            (1024, 2, 800.0, 1800.0, 60.0),
            (2048, 1, 1000.0, 2000.0, 130.0),
        ]

        for Nvec, max_starts, beta_low, beta_high, tbase in vector_schedule:
            if time.monotonic() >= work_deadline - 20.0:
                break
            starts = []

            if best_p is not None:
                starts.append(_upsample_p(best_p, Nvec))

            parN = _parent_p(Nvec)
            if parN is not None:
                starts.append(parN)

            starts.append(_init_power_p(Nvec, 0.75, False))
            starts.append(_init_power_p(Nvec, 1.50, True))
            starts.append(_init_exp_p(Nvec, 1.8, False))
            starts.append(_init_exp_p(Nvec, 2.5, True))
            starts.append(_init_step_p(Nvec, 16, "linear2"))
            starts.append(_init_step_p(Nvec, 32, "pow05"))
            starts.append(_init_sine_p(Nvec, 1, 0.65, 0.7))
            starts.append(_init_sine_p(Nvec, 2, 0.5, 0.3))

            # Remove trivial duplicates by size and first few values.
            uniq = []
            seen = set()
            for p0 in starts:
                p0 = np.asarray(p0, dtype=float)
                p0 = np.nan_to_num(p0, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
                p0 = np.maximum(p0, 0.0)
                s = float(np.sum(p0))
                if p0.size != Nvec or s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
                    continue
                p0 = p0 / s
                key = (Nvec,) + tuple(np.round(p0[:12], 5).tolist())
                if key in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(key)
                uniq.append(p0)

            p0_list = uniq[:max_starts]
            for p0 in p0_list:
                if time.monotonic() >= work_deadline - 15.0:
                    break
                q0 = np.log(p0 + 1e-300)
                q0 -= float(np.mean(q0))

                rem = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
                timeout = min(tbase, max(1.5, rem - 8.0))
                x1 = _run_vector_lbfgs(q0, Nvec, beta_low, 50, timeout)
                p1 = _softmax(x1)
                p1 = p1 / float(np.sum(p1))
                _consider_p(p1)

                if time.monotonic() < work_deadline - 20.0:
                    rem2 = work_deadline - time.monotonic()
                    timeout2 = min(tbase * 0.8, max(1.5, rem2 - 8.0))
                    x2 = _run_vector_lbfgs(x1, Nvec, beta_high, 40, timeout2)
                    p2 = _softmax(x2)
                    p2 = p2 / float(np.sum(p2))
                    _consider_p(p2)

        print(f"[t={time.monotonic()-start:7.2f}] after vector LBFGS objective = {best_obj:.9f}, len={best_p.size if best_p is not None else 0}", flush=True)

    # Final refinement on a few useful lengths.
    if best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < work_deadline - 10.0:
        refine_lengths = {int(best_p.size)}
        for Nf in (256, 512, 1024, 2048):
            if Nf <= 4096:
                refine_lengths.add(Nf)
        refine_lengths = sorted(L for L in refine_lengths if 2 <= L <= 4096)

        for Nfin in refine_lengths:
            if time.monotonic() >= work_deadline - 12.0:
                break
            pfin = _upsample_p(best_p, Nfin)
            if Nfin <= 1024 and hard_deadline - time.monotonic() > 110.0:
                qfin = np.log(pfin + 1e-300)
                qfin -= float(np.mean(qfin))
                dl = min(hard_deadline - 25.0, time.monotonic() + min(90.0, max(10.0, (work_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.25)))
                _qfin, pfin, _fobj = _refine_q_subgrad(qfin, Nfin, 1500.0, 120, dl, rng)
            if hard_deadline - time.monotonic() > 35.0:
                dl2 = min(hard_deadline - 12.0, time.monotonic() + min(60.0, max(8.0, (work_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.25)))
                pfin, _objfin = _local_block_search(pfin, Nfin, rng, dl2)
            _consider_p(pfin)

        print(f"[t={time.monotonic()-start:7.2f}] after refinement objective = {best_obj:.9f}, len={best_p.size if best_p is not None else 0}", flush=True)

    # Build final candidate set and choose by internal objective, then verify a
    # couple with the exact evaluator.
    candidates = []
    if best_p is not None:
        candidates.append(best_p.copy())
    for psmall in small_ps:
        if len(candidates) < 12:
            candidates.append(psmall.copy())
    if best_p is not None:
        candidates.append(_upsample_p(best_p, 2048))
        if best_p.size <= 512:
            candidates.append(_upsample_p(best_p, 4096))

    final_internal_p = None
    final_internal_obj = np.inf
    for p in candidates:
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
        p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(p))
        if p.size == 0 or s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
            continue
        p = p / s
        obj = float(_p_exact_obj(p))
        if math.isfinite(obj) and obj < final_internal_obj:
            final_internal_p = p
            final_internal_obj = obj

    if final_internal_p is None:
        # Absolute fallback: uniform sequence.
        final_internal_p = np.ones(2048, dtype=float) / 2048.0
        final_internal_obj = float(_p_exact_obj(final_internal_p))

    # Official verification with at most a few exact evaluations.
    best_list = _p_to_list(final_internal_p)
    best_score = _official_score(best_list)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = final_internal_obj

    alt_candidates = []
    if final_internal_p.size != 2048:
        alt_candidates.append(_upsample_p(final_internal_p, 2048))
    elif time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 10.0:
        alt_candidates.append(_upsample_p(final_internal_p, 4096))

    for palt in alt_candidates:
        if time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline - 4.0:
            break
        palt = np.asarray(palt, dtype=float)
        palt = np.nan_to_num(palt, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        palt = np.maximum(palt, 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(palt))
        if palt.size == 0 or s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
            continue
        palt = palt / s
        a = _p_to_list(palt)
        sc = _official_score(a)
        if not math.isfinite(sc):
            sc = float(_p_exact_obj(palt))
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_list = a

    print(f"[t={time.monotonic()-start:7.2f}] returned length={len(best_list)}, internal_best={final_internal_obj:.9f}, official_best={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)
    return best_list
```
